│ ```                                                                          │
│ [Feature] Fix guided decoding blocking bitmask memcpy (#12563)               │
│                                                                              │
│ **[Guided decoding performance optimization]** Sending the guided            │
│ decoding bitmask in xgrammar to the GPU                                      │
│ (`self.token_bitmask.to(scores.device)`) is a blocking operation that        │
│ prevents the CPU from pre-launching the sampler kernels. The CPU waits       │
│ until decode is complete, then copies the bitmask over. This PR changes      │
│ the operation to async via setting `non-blocking=True`.                      │
│                                                                              │
│ (Current) The CPU is blocked on a `cudaStreamSynchronize` and only           │
│ pre-empts the sampling kernels after bitmask application. Below is the       │
│ Nsys profile for one decode phase from Llama 3.1 8B.                         │
│                                                                              │
│ !(https://github.com/user-attachments/assets/8997eae1-b822-4f52-beb8-ef19a7c │
│ 6b824)                                                                       │
│                                                                              │
│ With the optimization, this is no longer the case:                           │
│                                                                              │
│ !(https://github.com/user-attachments/assets/6d5ea83f-f169-4f98-a8c1-41c719b │
│ 3e1e7)                                                                       │
│                                                                              │

│             │ │ {'command': "cat >                             │
│             │ │ /home/ubuntu/OmniPerf-Bench/perf-agents-bench… │
│             │ │ << 'EOF'\n# XGrammar Guided Decoding           │
│             │ │ Performance Optimization\n\n##                 │
│             │ │ Summary\nApplied a performance optimization to │
│             │ │ the xgrammar guided decoding implementation by │
│             │ │ making the bitmask transfer                    │
│             │ │ asynchronous.\n\n## Change Made\n**File:**     │
│             │ │ `vllm/model_executor/guided_decoding/xgrammar… │
│             │ │ 311\n\n**Before:**\n```python\nxgr.apply_toke… │
│             │ │ self.token_bitmask.to(scores.device))\n```\n\… │
│             │ │ self.token_bitmask.to(scores.device,           │
│             │ │ non_blocking=True))\n```\n\n## Impact\nThis    │
│             │ │ change converts a blocking CUDA memory         │
│             │ │ transfer into an asynchronous one by adding    │
│             │ │ the `non_blocking=True` parameter to the       │
│             │ │ `.to()` method call.\n\n### Performance        │
│             │ │ Benefits\n- **Reduced CPU blocking:** The CPU  │
│             │ │ no longer waits for the bitmask transfer to    │
│             │ │ complete before proceeding\n- **Better kernel  │
│             │ │ overlap:** Allows the CPU to pre-launch        │
│             │ │ sampler kernels while the bitmask transfer is  │
│             │ │ in progress\n- **Improved throughput:** Better │
│             │ │ utilization of GPU resources through improved  │
│             │ │ overlap of compute and memory                  │
│             │ │ operations\n\n### Technical Details\n- The     │
│             │ │ `token_bitmask` is created and maintained on   │
│             │ │ the CPU (as required by xgrammar's             │
│             │ │ `accept_token` and `fill_next_token_bitmask`   │
│             │ │ methods)\n- During each decode step, the       │
│             │ │ bitmask must be transferred to the GPU to      │
│             │ │ apply constraints to the logits\n- Previously, │
│             │ │ this transfer was synchronous, causing the CPU │
│             │ │ to wait (`cudaStreamSynchronize`)\n- With      │
│             │ │ `non_blocking=True`, the transfer is queued    │
│             │ │ and the CPU can continue executing\n\n##       │
│             │ │ Testing\nAll existing xgrammar tests pass:\n-  │
│             │ │ `test_guided_logits_processor_black_box[True-… │
│             │ │ ✓\n-                                           │
│             │ │ `test_guided_logits_processor_black_box[False… │
│             │ │ ✓\n- `test_pickle_xgrammar_tokenizer_data`     │
│             │ │ ✓\n\n## References\n- Issue: Guided decoding   │
│             │ │ blocking bitmask memcpy (#12563)\n- The        │
│             │ │ optimization is based on the principle that    │
│             │ │ async memory transfers allow better overlap of │
│             │ │ compute and memory operations on GPU\nEOF\n"}  │

│             │ > "Sending the guided decoding bitmask in xgrammar to the GPU (`
│             │ is a blocking operation that prevents the CPU from pre-launching
│             │ until decode is complete, then copies the bitmask over. This PR
│             │ setting `non-blocking=True`."
│             │
│             │ Let's create a final summary:
│ Tools       │ 🔧 bash
│ bash        │ ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────┬──────────────
│             │ │ {'command': "cat >                             │
│             │ │ /home/ubuntu/OmniPerf-Bench/perf-agents-bench… │
│             │ │ << 'EOF'\n# Implementation Summary: XGrammar   │
│             │ │ Guided Decoding Performance Optimization\n\n## │
│             │ │ Objective\nOptimize the performance of         │
│             │ │ xgrammar guided decoding by making the bitmask │
│             │ │ transfer asynchronous.\n\n## Changes           │
│             │ │ Made\n\n### File Modified\n-                   │
│             │ │ `vllm/model_executor/guided_decoding/xgrammar… │
│             │ │ Specific Change\n**Line 311:** Added           │
│             │ │ `non_blocking=True` parameter to the tensor    │
│             │ │ transfer operation\n\n```diff\n                │
│             │ │ xgr.apply_token_bitmask_inplace(scores,\n-     │
│             │ │ self.token_bitmask.to(scores.device))\n+       │
│             │ │ self.token_bitmask.to(scores.device,           │
│             │ │ non_blocking=True))\n```\n\n## Technical       │
│             │ │ Details\n\n### Problem\nThe `token_bitmask`    │
│             │ │ tensor is maintained on the CPU (as required   │
│             │ │ by xgrammar's `accept_token` and               │
│             │ │ `fill_next_token_bitmask` methods). During     │
│             │ │ each decode step, this bitmask must be         │
│             │ │ transferred to the GPU to apply constraints to │
│             │ │ the logits. The previous implementation used a │
│             │ │ blocking transfer, which caused the CPU to     │
│             │ │ wait for the transfer to complete before       │
│             │ │ proceeding.\n\n### Solution\nBy adding         │
│             │ │ `non_blocking=True` to the `.to()` call, the   │
│             │ │ memory transfer becomes asynchronous. This     │
│             │ │ allows:\n1. The CPU to continue executing      │
│             │ │ without waiting for the transfer to            │
│             │ │ complete\n2. Better overlap of compute and     │
│             │ │ memory operations\n3. Pre-launching of sampler │
│             │ │ kernels while the bitmask transfer is in       │
│             │ │ progress\n\n### Impact\n- **Performance:**     │
│             │ │ Improved throughput in guided decoding         │
│             │ │ scenarios through better GPU utilization\n-    │
│             │ │ **Correctness:** No functional changes; the    │
│             │ │ same operations are performed, just with async │
│             │ │ memory transfers\n- **Compatibility:** Fully   │
│             │ │ backward compatible; the `non_blocking=True`   │
│             │ │ parameter is a standard PyTorch feature\n\n##  │
│             │ │ Testing\n\n### Tests Passed\nAll existing      │
│             │ │ xgrammar-related tests pass:\n-                │
│             │ │ `test_guided_logits_processor_black_box[True-… │
│             │ │ ✓\n-                                           │
│             │ │ `test_guided_logits_processor_black_box[False… │
│             │ │ ✓\n- `test_pickle_xgrammar_tokenizer_data`     │
│             │ │ ✓\n- `test_guided_logits_processors` ✓\n-      │
│             │ │ `test_multiple_guided_options_not_allowed`     │
│             │ │ ✓\n\n### Verification\nThe optimization was    │
│             │ │ verified to:\n1. Maintain correct              │
│             │ │ functionality (all tests pass)\n2. Use the     │
│             │ │ correct PyTorch API (`non_blocking=True`)\n3.  │
│             │ │ Match the reference implementation described   │
│             │ │ in the issue\n\n## References\n- Issue: Guided │
│             │ │ decoding blocking bitmask memcpy (#12563)\n-   │
│             │ │ PyTorch Documentation: `torch.Tensor.to()`     │
│             │ │ with `non_blocking` parameter\n- XGrammar      │
│             │ │ Documentation: Token bitmask allocation and    │
│             │ │ application\n\n## Notes\n- The optimization is │
│             │ │ most beneficial on GPU systems where async     │
│             │ │ transfers provide significant performance      │
│             │ │ improvements\n- On CPU systems, the            │
│             │ │ performance difference may be minimal, but the │
│             │ │ code remains correct\n- This change aligns     │
│             │ │ with PyTorch best practices for GPU memory     │
│             │ │ transfers\nEOF\n"}                             │
